> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

        Idea:
Add a user_actions concept to the finalize phase that extracts human-only setup/operational steps (env vars, secret keys, db migrations, manual UI smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals) out of the executor's task list and into a dedicated subdoc, with multiple enforcement layers so the agent actually respects the boundary.

Concrete design spec:

1. Schema (megaplan/schemas.py): add a top-level user_actions array to the finalize JSON schema, peer to tasks/watch_items/sense_checks. Each user_action object: id (e.g. U1), description, phase (before_execute or after_execute), blocks_task_ids (array of task IDs, optional), rationale, plus a requires_human_only_reason field that is required only when this user_action is the sole coverage for a plan step.

2. Coverage validation rename: in the finalize validation block, rename finalize_task_ids -> finalize_item_ids (covers both task IDs and user_action IDs). Update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py and the prompt in megaplan/prompts/finalize.py accordingly. Prevents silent-drop: every plan step covered by at least one task or user_action.

3. Finalize prompt (megaplan/prompts/finalize.py): add tight inclusion criteria for user_actions: ONLY env vars/secrets, infra access, db migrations, manual UI/UX smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals. Anything that touches code in the repo MUST be a task. Include positive and negative examples. Mode-gate: only inject in code mode; doc/joke/creative modes -> [].

4. Synthetic gate task injection (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): mirror existing _ensure_verification_task pattern with two new helpers:
   - _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task: if any before_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the head of tasks. Description must force programmatic verification: 'Read user_actions.md. For each before_execute action, programmatically verify completion using bash tools - grep .env for required keys, query the migrations table, curl the dev server, etc. Reading the file does NOT count as verification; you must run a command. For actions that genuinely cannot be verified mechanically (manual UI checks), explicitly ask the user. If anything is incomplete or unverifiable, mark this task blocked with reason and STOP.'
   - _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task: if any after_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the tail. Description: surfaces them clearly to the user post-execute.
   - Both add corresponding sense_checks. Both are no-ops if user_actions empty.

5. Per-task prerequisite injection (megaplan/prompts/execute.py and megaplan/execute/core.py): when building the executor prompt for task T_n, if any user_action lists T_n in blocks_task_ids, prepend a PREREQUISITE block: 'This task depends on user action U_x: <description>. If U_x is not complete (verify if possible), mark this task blocked with reason awaiting U_x rather than attempting it.' Belt-and-suspenders with T0 gate task.

6. Artifact rendering (megaplan/_core/io.py): render_final_md adds a User Actions section after sense_checks. Also write a separate user_actions.md subdoc. Per data-parity principle, always write user_actions.md even when empty (with stub) so file layout is consistent across modes/robustness.

7. Finalize handler wiring (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): _write_finalize_artifacts writes user_actions.md alongside final.md. Finalize summary message includes user_action count loudly when non-zero.

8. Validation (_validate_finalize_payload in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): require user_actions to be a list (may be empty). Each entry must have id, description, phase. Reject if requires_human_only_reason is missing on a user_action that is the sole finalize_item_ids entry for any plan step.

9. Tests: extend finalize tests. Specifically: schema accepts new user_actions field; _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task injects T0 with verification-forcing language; _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task injects tail task; both no-ops when empty; finalize_item_ids rename covers both; user_actions.md written even when empty; doc/joke/creative modes produce empty user_actions.

OUT OF SCOPE: reviewer cross-check, hard execute gate state, bakeoff comparator changes, DAG scheduler. Synthetic T0 task IS the gate via executor.

Key safety bet: synthetic T0 verification task converts user_actions from passive documentation into enforcement. Existing precedent to mirror: _ensure_verification_task in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: T1 (schema) is the foundation \u2014 both because its `user_actions` shape and the `finalize_task_ids` \u2192 `finalize_item_ids` rename are referenced by every downstream task, and because the FLAG-001 fix lives entirely in T1 (the `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` marker on the items schema). T2 (prompt) and T3 (validation) follow T1 and can be done together; both also depend on the rename. T4 (synthetic gates) depends on T1 (schema for user_actions) and T3 (validation must accept the new shape) \u2014 and the in-handler order is critical: in `_write_finalize_artifacts` invoke `_ensure_verification_task` first (existing), then `_ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task`, then `_ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task` LAST so the post-gate computes its terminal set over the final task graph. T5 (rendering) depends on T1 (the field exists) and T4 (so synthetic gates are present in final.md). T6 (executor PREREQUISITE) only needs T1 (schema). T7 (tests) closes the loop, then T8 runs the suite plus a throwaway repro script.\\n\\nCritical correctness traps to avoid: (1) Forgetting `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` on `user_actions.items` \u2014 the existing precedent on tasks items (schemas.py:309) is what allows tasks to declare a narrower `required` than its full property set; without it, strict_schema() at :752 widens `required` to every property key and forces requires_human_only_reason on every populated user_action. T7's case (1) and (6) explicitly guard this \u2014 run them locally as a smoke. (2) Letting the post-gate depend only on the previous last task \u2014 `compute_task_batches` at _core/io.py:54-99 is dependency-edge driven, so a branched graph (e.g. T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3) would let the post-gate run before T3 finishes. Compute terminals as `task_ids - {dep for t in tasks for dep in t['depends_on']}`. (3) Not prepending the pre-gate ID to existing tasks' depends_on \u2014 list position alone won't enforce ordering. Prepend to every other task, including those that already had deps. (4) Forgetting to update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation for both the rename (read `finalize_item_ids` only) AND auto-covering the synthetic gate tasks (otherwise they'll be flagged as orphans and pollute validation output).\\n\\nMode-gating logic: use `state['config'].get('mode','code') == 'code'` consistently. Non-code modes must emit empty user_actions, must skip both gate helpers, and must STILL write the `user_actions.md` stub (data-parity per the user's existing memory).\\n\\nThe `requires_human_only_reason` rule is conditional, not unconditional \u2014 required only when the user_action is the SOLE finalize_item_id for any plan step. The schema keeps it optional via the strict-schema marker; the handler validator enforces the conditional requirement at validate-time. Don't accidentally make it always-required at the schema level.\\n\\nFor the executor prompt prerequisite injection (T6), do NOT touch megaplan/execute/core.py \u2014 the `batch_prerequisites` field at :651 is for unrelated batch ordering. The injection is purely in `_execute_batch_prompt` in megaplan/prompts/execute.py.\\n\\nWhen running tests in T8, expect `tests/test_finalize.py:11` and adjacent fixtures to need updating because they hard-code `finalize_task_ids`. Update them, don't add a compat shim. The grep step is the proof that the rename was complete.\\n\\nThe throwaway script in T8 is a real e2e check: build a minimal payload with both before_execute and after_execute user_actions and a branched task graph (T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3), call `_write_finalize_artifacts` against a tmp plan_dir, and assert the four observable outcomes (pre-gate at index 0, terminal-set deps on post-gate, user_actions.md present with both sections, final.md has `## User Actions`). This cross-checks unit tests against the actual handler integration. Delete the script after.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname="hidden md:block">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls. > DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

        Idea:
Add a user_actions concept to the finalize phase that extracts human-only setup/operational steps (env vars, secret keys, db migrations, manual UI smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals) out of the executor's task list and into a dedicated subdoc, with multiple enforcement layers so the agent actually respects the boundary.

Concrete design spec:

1. Schema (megaplan/schemas.py): add a top-level user_actions array to the finalize JSON schema, peer to tasks/watch_items/sense_checks. Each user_action object: id (e.g. U1), description, phase (before_execute or after_execute), blocks_task_ids (array of task IDs, optional), rationale, plus a requires_human_only_reason field that is required only when this user_action is the sole coverage for a plan step.

2. Coverage validation rename: in the finalize validation block, rename finalize_task_ids -> finalize_item_ids (covers both task IDs and user_action IDs). Update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py and the prompt in megaplan/prompts/finalize.py accordingly. Prevents silent-drop: every plan step covered by at least one task or user_action.

3. Finalize prompt (megaplan/prompts/finalize.py): add tight inclusion criteria for user_actions: ONLY env vars/secrets, infra access, db migrations, manual UI/UX smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals. Anything that touches code in the repo MUST be a task. Include positive and negative examples. Mode-gate: only inject in code mode; doc/joke/creative modes -> [].

4. Synthetic gate task injection (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): mirror existing _ensure_verification_task pattern with two new helpers:
   - _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task: if any before_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the head of tasks. Description must force programmatic verification: 'Read user_actions.md. For each before_execute action, programmatically verify completion using bash tools - grep .env for required keys, query the migrations table, curl the dev server, etc. Reading the file does NOT count as verification; you must run a command. For actions that genuinely cannot be verified mechanically (manual UI checks), explicitly ask the user. If anything is incomplete or unverifiable, mark this task blocked with reason and STOP.'
   - _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task: if any after_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the tail. Description: surfaces them clearly to the user post-execute.
   - Both add corresponding sense_checks. Both are no-ops if user_actions empty.

5. Per-task prerequisite injection (megaplan/prompts/execute.py and megaplan/execute/core.py): when building the executor prompt for task T_n, if any user_action lists T_n in blocks_task_ids, prepend a PREREQUISITE block: 'This task depends on user action U_x: <description>. If U_x is not complete (verify if possible), mark this task blocked with reason awaiting U_x rather than attempting it.' Belt-and-suspenders with T0 gate task.

6. Artifact rendering (megaplan/_core/io.py): render_final_md adds a User Actions section after sense_checks. Also write a separate user_actions.md subdoc. Per data-parity principle, always write user_actions.md even when empty (with stub) so file layout is consistent across modes/robustness.

7. Finalize handler wiring (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): _write_finalize_artifacts writes user_actions.md alongside final.md. Finalize summary message includes user_action count loudly when non-zero.

8. Validation (_validate_finalize_payload in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): require user_actions to be a list (may be empty). Each entry must have id, description, phase. Reject if requires_human_only_reason is missing on a user_action that is the sole finalize_item_ids entry for any plan step.

9. Tests: extend finalize tests. Specifically: schema accepts new user_actions field; _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task injects T0 with verification-forcing language; _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task injects tail task; both no-ops when empty; finalize_item_ids rename covers both; user_actions.md written even when empty; doc/joke/creative modes produce empty user_actions.

OUT OF SCOPE: reviewer cross-check, hard execute gate state, bakeoff comparator changes, DAG scheduler. Synthetic T0 task IS the gate via executor.

Key safety bet: synthetic T0 verification task converts user_actions from passive documentation into enforcement. Existing precedent to mirror: _ensure_verification_task in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: T1 (schema) is the foundation \u2014 both because its `user_actions` shape and the `finalize_task_ids` \u2192 `finalize_item_ids` rename are referenced by every downstream task, and because the FLAG-001 fix lives entirely in T1 (the `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` marker on the items schema). T2 (prompt) and T3 (validation) follow T1 and can be done together; both also depend on the rename. T4 (synthetic gates) depends on T1 (schema for user_actions) and T3 (validation must accept the new shape) \u2014 and the in-handler order is critical: in `_write_finalize_artifacts` invoke `_ensure_verification_task` first (existing), then `_ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task`, then `_ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task` LAST so the post-gate computes its terminal set over the final task graph. T5 (rendering) depends on T1 (the field exists) and T4 (so synthetic gates are present in final.md). T6 (executor PREREQUISITE) only needs T1 (schema). T7 (tests) closes the loop, then T8 runs the suite plus a throwaway repro script.\\n\\nCritical correctness traps to avoid: (1) Forgetting `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` on `user_actions.items` \u2014 the existing precedent on tasks items (schemas.py:309) is what allows tasks to declare a narrower `required` than its full property set; without it, strict_schema() at :752 widens `required` to every property key and forces requires_human_only_reason on every populated user_action. T7's case (1) and (6) explicitly guard this \u2014 run them locally as a smoke. (2) Letting the post-gate depend only on the previous last task \u2014 `compute_task_batches` at _core/io.py:54-99 is dependency-edge driven, so a branched graph (e.g. T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3) would let the post-gate run before T3 finishes. Compute terminals as `task_ids - {dep for t in tasks for dep in t['depends_on']}`. (3) Not prepending the pre-gate ID to existing tasks' depends_on \u2014 list position alone won't enforce ordering. Prepend to every other task, including those that already had deps. (4) Forgetting to update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation for both the rename (read `finalize_item_ids` only) AND auto-covering the synthetic gate tasks (otherwise they'll be flagged as orphans and pollute validation output).\\n\\nMode-gating logic: use `state['config'].get('mode','code') == 'code'` consistently. Non-code modes must emit empty user_actions, must skip both gate helpers, and must STILL write the `user_actions.md` stub (data-parity per the user's existing memory).\\n\\nThe `requires_human_only_reason` rule is conditional, not unconditional \u2014 required only when the user_action is the SOLE finalize_item_id for any plan step. The schema keeps it optional via the strict-schema marker; the handler validator enforces the conditional requirement at validate-time. Don't accidentally make it always-required at the schema level.\\n\\nFor the executor prompt prerequisite injection (T6), do NOT touch megaplan/execute/core.py \u2014 the `batch_prerequisites` field at :651 is for unrelated batch ordering. The injection is purely in `_execute_batch_prompt` in megaplan/prompts/execute.py.\\n\\nWhen running tests in T8, expect `tests/test_finalize.py:11` and adjacent fixtures to need updating because they hard-code `finalize_task_ids`. Update them, don't add a compat shim. The grep step is the proof that the rename was complete.\\n\\nThe throwaway script in T8 is a real e2e check: build a minimal payload with both before_execute and after_execute user_actions and a branched task graph (T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3), call `_write_finalize_artifacts` against a tmp plan_dir, and assert the four observable outcomes (pre-gate at index 0, terminal-set deps on post-gate, user_actions.md present with both sections, final.md has `## User Actions`). This cross-checks unit tests against the actual handler integration. Delete the script after.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname="hidden md:block">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

These modes must not emit human-only user_actions.\n+            \"\"\"\n+        ).strip()\n     return textwrap.dedent(\n         f\"\"\"\n         You are preparing an execution-ready briefing document from the approved plan.\n@@\n         - `watch_items` must be an array of strings covering runtime risks, critique concerns, and assumptions to keep visible during execution.\n         - `sense_checks` must be an array with one verification question per task. Every sense-check object must include:\n           - `id`: short stable ID like `SC1`\n           - `task_id`: the related task ID\n           - `question`: reviewer verification question\n           - `verdict`: always `\"\"` at finalize time\n+        - {user_actions_guidance}\n         - `meta_commentary` must be a single string with execution guidance, gotchas, or judgment calls that help the executor succeed.\n         - `validation` must be an object that self-checks plan coverage:\n-          - `plan_steps_covered`: enumerate EVERY step from the approved plan. For each step, provide a short `plan_step_summary` (the step's intent in one phrase) and `finalize_task_ids` (array of task IDs that implement it — a single plan step may map to multiple tasks).\n+          - `plan_steps_covered`: enumerate EVERY step from the approved plan. For each step, provide a short `plan_step_summary` (the step's intent in one phrase) and `finalize_item_ids` (array of task IDs `T*` or user_action IDs `U*` that implement or cover it — a single plan step may map to multiple tasks and/or user_actions).\n           - `orphan_tasks`: task IDs that do not correspond to any plan step. Normally empty. If non-empty, explain in `completeness_notes`.\n           - `completeness_notes`: free-text explanation of any gaps, deviations, or deliberate omissions.\n           - `coverage_complete`: set to `true` only if every plan step has at least one finalize task AND you have verified the mapping by reviewing each entry. Set to `false` if any plan step is missing coverage.\n           - Example:\n           ```json\n           \"validation\": {{\n             \"plan_steps_covered\": [\n-              {{\"plan_step_summary\": \"Add retry logic to API client\", \"finalize_task_ids\": [\"T1\", \"T2\"]}},\n-              {{\"plan_step_summary\": \"Update configuration schema\", \"finalize_task_ids\": [\"T3\"]}}\n+              {{\"plan_step_summary\": \"Add retry logic to API client\", \"finalize_item_ids\": [\"T1\", \"T2\"]}},\n+              {{\"plan_step_summary\": \"Set required production secret\", \"finalize_item_ids\": [\"U1\"]}}\n             ],\n             \"orphan_tasks\": [],\n             \"completeness_notes\": \"All plan steps mapped to tasks.\",\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

        Idea:
Add a user_actions concept to the finalize phase that extracts human-only setup/operational steps (env vars, secret keys, db migrations, manual UI smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals) out of the executor's task list and into a dedicated subdoc, with multiple enforcement layers so the agent actually respects the boundary.

Concrete design spec:

1. Schema (megaplan/schemas.py): add a top-level user_actions array to the finalize JSON schema, peer to tasks/watch_items/sense_checks. Each user_action object: id (e.g. U1), description, phase (before_execute or after_execute), blocks_task_ids (array of task IDs, optional), rationale, plus a requires_human_only_reason field that is required only when this user_action is the sole coverage for a plan step.

2. Coverage validation rename: in the finalize validation block, rename finalize_task_ids -> finalize_item_ids (covers both task IDs and user_action IDs). Update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py and the prompt in megaplan/prompts/finalize.py accordingly. Prevents silent-drop: every plan step covered by at least one task or user_action.

3. Finalize prompt (megaplan/prompts/finalize.py): add tight inclusion criteria for user_actions: ONLY env vars/secrets, infra access, db migrations, manual UI/UX smoke tests, deploys, out-of-band approvals. Anything that touches code in the repo MUST be a task. Include positive and negative examples. Mode-gate: only inject in code mode; doc/joke/creative modes -> [].

4. Synthetic gate task injection (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): mirror existing _ensure_verification_task pattern with two new helpers:
   - _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task: if any before_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the head of tasks. Description must force programmatic verification: 'Read user_actions.md. For each before_execute action, programmatically verify completion using bash tools - grep .env for required keys, query the migrations table, curl the dev server, etc. Reading the file does NOT count as verification; you must run a command. For actions that genuinely cannot be verified mechanically (manual UI checks), explicitly ask the user. If anything is incomplete or unverifiable, mark this task blocked with reason and STOP.'
   - _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task: if any after_execute user_actions exist, INJECT a synthetic task at the tail. Description: surfaces them clearly to the user post-execute.
   - Both add corresponding sense_checks. Both are no-ops if user_actions empty.

5. Per-task prerequisite injection (megaplan/prompts/execute.py and megaplan/execute/core.py): when building the executor prompt for task T_n, if any user_action lists T_n in blocks_task_ids, prepend a PREREQUISITE block: 'This task depends on user action U_x: <description>. If U_x is not complete (verify if possible), mark this task blocked with reason awaiting U_x rather than attempting it.' Belt-and-suspenders with T0 gate task.

6. Artifact rendering (megaplan/_core/io.py): render_final_md adds a User Actions section after sense_checks. Also write a separate user_actions.md subdoc. Per data-parity principle, always write user_actions.md even when empty (with stub) so file layout is consistent across modes/robustness.

7. Finalize handler wiring (megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): _write_finalize_artifacts writes user_actions.md alongside final.md. Finalize summary message includes user_action count loudly when non-zero.

8. Validation (_validate_finalize_payload in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py): require user_actions to be a list (may be empty). Each entry must have id, description, phase. Reject if requires_human_only_reason is missing on a user_action that is the sole finalize_item_ids entry for any plan step.

9. Tests: extend finalize tests. Specifically: schema accepts new user_actions field; _ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task injects T0 with verification-forcing language; _ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task injects tail task; both no-ops when empty; finalize_item_ids rename covers both; user_actions.md written even when empty; doc/joke/creative modes produce empty user_actions.

OUT OF SCOPE: reviewer cross-check, hard execute gate state, bakeoff comparator changes, DAG scheduler. Synthetic T0 task IS the gate via executor.

Key safety bet: synthetic T0 verification task converts user_actions from passive documentation into enforcement. Existing precedent to mirror: _ensure_verification_task in megaplan/handlers/finalize.py.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution order is load-bearing: T1 (schema) is the foundation \u2014 both because its `user_actions` shape and the `finalize_task_ids` \u2192 `finalize_item_ids` rename are referenced by every downstream task, and because the FLAG-001 fix lives entirely in T1 (the `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` marker on the items schema). T2 (prompt) and T3 (validation) follow T1 and can be done together; both also depend on the rename. T4 (synthetic gates) depends on T1 (schema for user_actions) and T3 (validation must accept the new shape) \u2014 and the in-handler order is critical: in `_write_finalize_artifacts` invoke `_ensure_verification_task` first (existing), then `_ensure_user_actions_pre_gate_task`, then `_ensure_user_actions_post_gate_task` LAST so the post-gate computes its terminal set over the final task graph. T5 (rendering) depends on T1 (the field exists) and T4 (so synthetic gates are present in final.md). T6 (executor PREREQUISITE) only needs T1 (schema). T7 (tests) closes the loop, then T8 runs the suite plus a throwaway repro script.\\n\\nCritical correctness traps to avoid: (1) Forgetting `x-preserve-explicit-required: True` on `user_actions.items` \u2014 the existing precedent on tasks items (schemas.py:309) is what allows tasks to declare a narrower `required` than its full property set; without it, strict_schema() at :752 widens `required` to every property key and forces requires_human_only_reason on every populated user_action. T7's case (1) and (6) explicitly guard this \u2014 run them locally as a smoke. (2) Letting the post-gate depend only on the previous last task \u2014 `compute_task_batches` at _core/io.py:54-99 is dependency-edge driven, so a branched graph (e.g. T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3) would let the post-gate run before T3 finishes. Compute terminals as `task_ids - {dep for t in tasks for dep in t['depends_on']}`. (3) Not prepending the pre-gate ID to existing tasks' depends_on \u2014 list position alone won't enforce ordering. Prepend to every other task, including those that already had deps. (4) Forgetting to update _reconcile_validation_after_mutation for both the rename (read `finalize_item_ids` only) AND auto-covering the synthetic gate tasks (otherwise they'll be flagged as orphans and pollute validation output).\\n\\nMode-gating logic: use `state['config'].get('mode','code') == 'code'` consistently. Non-code modes must emit empty user_actions, must skip both gate helpers, and must STILL write the `user_actions.md` stub (data-parity per the user's existing memory).\\n\\nThe `requires_human_only_reason` rule is conditional, not unconditional \u2014 required only when the user_action is the SOLE finalize_item_id for any plan step. The schema keeps it optional via the strict-schema marker; the handler validator enforces the conditional requirement at validate-time. Don't accidentally make it always-required at the schema level.\\n\\nFor the executor prompt prerequisite injection (T6), do NOT touch megaplan/execute/core.py \u2014 the `batch_prerequisites` field at :651 is for unrelated batch ordering. The injection is purely in `_execute_batch_prompt` in megaplan/prompts/execute.py.\\n\\nWhen running tests in T8, expect `tests/test_finalize.py:11` and adjacent fixtures to need updating because they hard-code `finalize_task_ids`. Update them, don't add a compat shim. The grep step is the proof that the rename was complete.\\n\\nThe throwaway script in T8 is a real e2e check: build a minimal payload with both before_execute and after_execute user_actions and a branched task graph (T1\u2192T2, T1\u2192T3), call `_write_finalize_artifacts` against a tmp plan_dir, and assert the four observable outcomes (pre-gate at index 0, terminal-set deps on post-gate, user_actions.md present with both sections, final.md has `## User Actions`). This cross-checks unit tests against the actual handler integration. Delete the script after.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname="hidden md:block">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: i checked the sign-in callback path again. `submissionslayout` already wires `usescoring(..., { onrequiresignin: () => pagemachine.dispatch({ type: 'open_sign_in_modal' }) })` at `src/pages/submissionslayout.tsx:38-40`, and `scorepanel` still has its own `onrequiresignin` prop. the revised step 4.2 is acceptable only if it explicitly rewires that prop to `pagemachine.dispatch`; otherwise removing local modal state would orphan the direct ui callback. the plan hints at this, but it remains easy to misread because the scoring-context callback and the scorepanel prop are separate mechanisms. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new diff-helper proposal against the repo's existing execution-evidence semantics. `validate_execution_evidence()` in `megaplan/evaluation.py:109-175` uses `git status --short`, so untracked files count as real changed files today, but step 3 proposes `collect_git_diff_patch(project_dir)` via `git diff head`; by git behavior that will not include untracked files, so heavy review could miss exactly the newly created files that execute and review already treat as part of the patch. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: a generated-schema gap remains. `tests/test_schemas.py` reads the repo-root `.megaplan/schemas/review.json` directly via `_review_disk_schema()`, the checked-in local file is currently stale relative to `schemas`, and `ensure_runtime_layout()` in `megaplan/_core/io.py` is the production writer for that file class. the plan says generated schemas must stay aligned, but it never names a regeneration step or a test adjustment for that disk-schema path, so the validation story is still incomplete. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new pre-extraction step that migrates `pinnedshotgroups` out of core is still under-specified for compatibility. current runtime and test code reads `config.pinnedshotgroups` directly across `hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts`, `lib/pinned-group-projection.ts`, `hooks/useshotgroups.ts`, `components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx`, `hooks/usetimelinetrackmanagement.ts`, `lib/serialize.test.ts`, `lib/migrate.test.ts`, and many other tests, so landing that migration before extraction needs a dual-read/projection shim or a clearly synchronized update plan instead of only a schema/serializer note. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: runner inputs: the revised auto-mode snippet calls `$(cat ${idea_file})` and the chain-mode path runs `mp-chain ${chain_spec}`, but step 7's deploy flow still only does `railway link`, `railway variables --set`, and `railway up`. there is no corresponding step that copies the idea file or chain spec into `/workspace`, so a literal implementation would boot into auto/chain mode and immediately fail on missing input files. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
